Adam optimizer details:
- Input y (log a mean-zero). We'll set a = exp(y). Because scale invariant, gradient gy should sum ~0 but we center.
- We'll use Adam with `lr` initial maybe 0.5? Need normalize gy. Let `g = gy - gy.mean()`. Normalize by RMS: `g_norm = np.linalg.norm(g) / np.sqrt(n)`. If g_norm>0, `g_scale = g / g_norm`. Then if using Adam, m/v operate on g_scale maybe. But adapting to scale.
- If we use normalized gradient, step size independent. Use simple Adam:
```
m = 0.9*m +0.1*g_scale
v=0.999*v+0.001*g_scale^2
step = lr * m/(sqrt(v)+1e-8)
y = y - step
```

Step size: We can use line search on exact score with y update. Since F scale invariant and smooth-ish, maybe use conjugate? Let's design a robust update:
```
lr = 0.5  # initial y step RMS approx
m=0; v=0
for iterations:
   compute b, Fa, max_b, S, score
   if score < best_score: update best
   if time > ... break
   beta_abs = 0.02 * max_b
   w = softmax(b, beta_abs)
   m_w = dot(w,b)
   Fw, c, G
   grad = (2*n/S**2)*(G - 2*m_w/S)
   gy = a*grad
   gy -= gy.mean()
   rms = np.linalg.norm(gy)/np.sqrt(n)
   if rms < 1e-12: break
   g = gy/rms
   # Adam
   m = 0.9*m + 0.1*g
   v = 0.999*v + 0.001*g*g
   mh = m/(1-0.9*iter)
   vh = v/(1-0.999*iter)
   step = lr * mh/(np.sqrt(vh)+1e-8)
   step -= step.mean()
   # maybe cap step norm
   step_rms = np.linalg.norm(step)/np.sqrt(n)
   max_step = 0.5 # y RMS
   if step_rms > max_step: step *= max_step/step_rms
   # line search: try y-candidate = y - step * factor? Since step already includes lr.
   accepted=False
   for factor in [1.0,0.5,0.25,0.1,0.05]:
       y_try = y - step*factor
       normalize_y(y_try)
       score_try = score_fast(y_try)
       if score_try < score - 1e-12 or score_try < best_score:
          y=y_try; score=score_try; accept; break
   if not accepted:
       # maybe smaller
       if lr>1e-4: lr *=0.7
       # else reset moment
   else:
       # keep lr or increase slightly if many accept
```

**Softmax weights and FFT gradient**
```
def softmax_weights(b, beta):
    m = b.max()
    # avoid overflow: x=(b-m)/beta
    x = (b - m) / beta
    # clip x to [-50, 0]? If beta small, many -inf
    x = np.clip(x, -50.0, 0.0)
    w = np.exp(x)
    s = w.sum()
    if s == 0 or not finite: w = zeros; w[argmax]=1
    else w /= s
    return w, w.dot(b) # w weighted b? Actually m_w = weighted sum b = m + beta * logsumexp(x) /? With x clipped, approximate.
```
If beta very small, w concentrated. If x clipped to -50, weights for far below 50 beta ignored. Good. β choices maybe 0.1? For n=4806, b max1, tail values drop by maybe O(1/√n)=0.01, so β=0.01 includes tail. Use β anneal: start 0.2 to global, end 0.01. But if β too large, m_w lower, gradient maybe favors increasing sum without focusing max? Still okay maybe. We can use β_rel list.

**FFT correlation function**
```
def fft_correlate_valid_w_a(w, a, pad=None, Fa=None):
    # ensure pad len >= 3n-2
    L=len(w); n=len(a)
    if pad is None: pad = 1 << (L+n-2).bit_length()
    Fw = np.fft.rfft(w, pad)
    if Fa is None: Fa = np.fft.rfft(a, pad)
    y = np.fft.irfft(Fa * Fw, pad)
    # y length pad, conv length L+n-1; need c indices as above
    start = L-1
    stop = L-n-1  # exclusive in negative direction
    c = y[start:stop:-1]
    # c length n? Check if stop may be negative? L-n-1 = n-2. For n=1? Handle n=1 separately.
    return c[:n]
```
Need ensure if stop negative? L=2n-1, L-n-1=n-2 ≥0 for n≥2; for n=1, L=1, start0, stop=-2, y[0:-2:-1] gives? Handle n=1 separately. Convolution length 3n-2, pad big enough; y values beyond length zero.
But using `Fa` computed with pad length from previous. Good.

**score_from_a**
```
def score_from_a(a):
    n=len(a); S=a.sum()
    if S <= 1e-12: return inf
    if n < 256: b=np.convolve(a,a)
    else: pad=1 << (2*n -2).bit_length(); Fa=np.fft.rfft(a,pad); b=np.fft.irfft(Fa*Fa,pad)[:2*n-1]
    b = np.real(b) # maybe small neg
    maxb=b.max()
    return 2*n*maxb/(S*S), maxb
```
For gradient, compute Fa once and b. Need if b has small negative due FFT, max maybe unaffected. For active weights, use b clipped to 0? Convolution nonnegative; FFT numerical may have negative tiny. Set `b = np.maximum(b, 0)`? But if negative large due error? Use `b = b.real; b[np.abs(b)<1e-12*max]=0`. Clipping negative to 0 may alter gradient slightly. Fine.

**Adam optimizer function**
Top-level:
```
def optimize_n(n, budget_s, rng, beta_start, beta_end, lr0, max_iterations=None, stop_score=None):
    # init a binomial normalized? y=log(a)
    a = binomial_coeffs(n)
    # maybe scale a.max to 1? y mean zero
    y = make_y_from_a(a)
    best_local_a = a.copy(); best_local_score=inf
    m=np.zeros(n); v=np.zeros(n)
    lr=lr0
    time_start...
    iter=0
    while time.monotonic()<deadline:
        a=np.exp(y)
        # compute b,Fa,maxb,S,score
        ...
        if score < best_local_score: update best
        if stop_score and best_local_score < stop_score: maybe continue? (don't stop global)
        # choose beta = beta_start + (beta_end-beta_start)*progress maybe
        beta = max(1e-8, beta_rel * maxb)
        w, mw = softmax_weights(b,beta)
        c = correlate from Fa,w
        G=2*c
        grad = (2*n/(S*S))*(G - 2*mw/S)
        gy = a*grad
        gy -= gy.mean()
        rms=np.linalg.norm(gy)/sqrt(n)
        if rms<1e-14: break
        g=gy/rms
        # Adam
        m=...; v=...
        step = lr * mu_hat/(sqrt(vhat)+1e-8)
        step -= step.mean()
        # cap step RMS to max_step (e.g., 0.35)
        step_rms=...
        if step_rms > max_step: step *= max_step/step_rms
        # candidate
        a_try = a * np.exp(-step)
        # ensure positive and finite; if nan break
        score_try = score_from_a(a_try)[0]
        if finite and (score_try < score or score_try < best_local_score or score_try < score*1.02):
            y = log(a_try); y=normalize_y(y)
            score = score_try
            # if score_try < best*: best
            stale=0; lr = min(1.0, lr*1.01)
        else:
            stale +=1
            if score_try > score*1.05:
                lr *=0.7
                if stale>15: lr=lr0*0.5; stale=0
            else:
                # accept small worsening for exploration? maybe if random
                if rng.random()<0.1 and score_try < score*1.01:
                    y=loga_try; score=score_try; stale=0
        iter++
    return best_local_a,best_local_score
```

**Softmax stable**:
```
def softmax_w(b, beta):
    m = b.max()
    if not finite(m): ...
    beta = max(beta, m*1e-12) if m>0 else 1e-12
    x=(b-m)/beta
    x=np.minimum(x,0.0)
    x=np.maximum(x,-50.0)
    w=np.exp(x)
    s=w.sum()
    if s<=0: w=sum; w[argmax]=1
    else w/=s
    mw = float(w.dot(b))
    return w,mw
```
If m=0, all zero sequence invalid; return uniform? But S>0. If beta relative to m.

**Convolution with FFT function**:
```
def fft_stats(a):
    n=a.size
    pad=1 << (3*n).bit_length()? For b only 2n, but gradient y needs 3n. In optimizer use pad3 = 1 << (3*n - 2).bit_length(). For small n, 3*n-2 bit.
    Fa = np.fft.rfft(a, pad)
    Bfa = Fa*Fa
    b = np.fft.irfft(Bfa, pad)
    Lb=2*n-1
    b = b[:Lb].copy()
    b = np.real_if_close(b)
    b = np.maximum(b,0.0)
    maxb=float(b.max())
    S=float(a.sum())
    score=2.0*n*maxb/(S*S) if S>0 else inf
    return Fa,b,maxb,S,score,pad
```
Using same pad for b and correlation. If a normalized max1, b values O(1); clip negative.
For `score_from_a(a, pad=None)` could compute shorter pad for score but use same pad3 for simplicity.

**Correlation `fft_gap_gradient`**:

def get_G_from_Fa_w(Fa, w, pad, n):
    L=2*n-1
    Fw=np.fft.rfft(w, pad)
    y=np.fft.irfft(Fa*Fw, pad)
    start=L-1
    stop=L-n-1
    c=y[start:stop:-1]
    # if length < n due weird, pad
    if c.size < n: c=np.pad(c,(0,n-c.size))
    return 2.0*c[:n]
```

**Adam with normalized gradient and step cap**:
```
mu_beta=0.9, v_beta=0.999
m = mu_beta*m + (1-mu_beta)*g
v = v_beta*v + (1-v_beta)*g*g
bias1=1-mu_beta**t; bias2=1-v_beta**t
mh=m/bias1; vh=v/bias2
raw = lr * mh/(np.sqrt(vh)+1e-8)
# Since g normalized RMS1, raw RMS ~lr*? but Adam can amplify. Cap RMS.

def optimize_n(n, budget_s, seed_list, rng, global_best_score):
    if n<=1: ...
    deadline = time.monotonic()+budget_s
    # choose initial seed
    if not seed_list: seed_list=[make_y_from_a(binomial_a(n))]
    seed_idx=0
    y = np.array(seed_list[0], dtype=float); normalize_y
    a=np.exp(y)
    # normalize a to max1? Seed y may already, but ensure:
    score, maxb, S = score_max_from_a(a)
    if maxb>0: a=a/np.sqrt(maxb); y=make_y_from_a(a)
    best_a=a.copy(); best_score=score
    m=np.zeros(n); v=np.zeros(n); lr=0.2; max_step=0.25; it=0; no_improve=0; last_print=...
    beta_start=0.05; beta_end=0.005
    while time<deadline:
       # compute stats
       Fa,b,maxb,S,score,pad = fft_stats(a)  # a max1 ideally
       if not finite score: break
       if score < best_score - 1e-12:
           best_score=score; best_a=a.copy(); no_improve=0
           if best_score < global_best_score? can't update global; return best
       # if current much worse than best maybe reset to best? We can if score>best*1.1 and it>... reset y=best_y, a=best_a.
       # gradient
       beta = ...
       w,mw=softmax...
       G=...
       grad = (2*n/(S*S))*(G - 2*mw/S)
       gy=a*grad; gy-=gy.mean()
       rms=...
       if rms<1e-14: break
       g=gy/rms
       it+=1; m=...; v=...
       step=lr*mh/(sqrt(vh)+1e-8); step-=step.mean(); cap
       # trial loop
       accepted=False
       for factor in [1.0,0.5]:
          a_try=a*np.exp(-step*factor)
          # ensure finite
          if not finite: continue
          score_try,maxb_try = score_max_from_a(a_try, pad)
          if finite:
             # normalize accept
             if maxb_try>0: a_try=a_try/np.sqrt(maxb_try)
             if score_try < score + 1e-12 or score_try < best_score or (score_try < score*1.02 and rng.random()<0.05):
                 y=make_y_from_a(a_try); a=a_try; score=score_try; accepted=True; break
       if accepted:
          no_improve = 0 if score < best_score - 1e-9 else no_improve+1
          if score < best_score: no_improve=0
          # adapt lr
          if score < current? ...
          # if many no_improve, reduce beta? (beta schedule anyway)
       else:
          no_improve +=1
          if lr > 0.02: lr *=0.8
          # if no_improve > 1000 and seed_idx+1 < len(seed_list) and time_left>300: reset seed
          if no_improve>3000 and len(seed_list)>seed_idx+1 and (deadline-time)>200:
              seed_idx+=1; y=np.array(seed_list[seed_idx]); normalize; a=exp; score...; m/v reset; lr=0.3; no_improve=0
          elif no_improve>5000:
              # add noise
              y = y + rng.normal(0,0.1,n); a=exp(y); normalize max1; score...; no_improve=0
       # print every it%500
    return best_a,best_score
```

**Direct `score_max_from_a` implementation**:
```
def score_max_from_a(a, pad=None):
    a = np.asarray(a,float)
    n=a.size
    if n==0: return inf,0
    S=float(a.sum())
    if S<=1e-300: return inf,0
    if n < 128:
        b=np.convolve(a,a)
    else:
        if pad is None: pad=1 << (3*n-2).bit_length()
        Fa=np.fft.rfft(a,pad)
        b=np.fft.irfft(Fa*Fa,pad)
        b=b[:2*n-1]
    b=np.real(b)
    maxb=float(b.max())
    if maxb < 0 and maxb > -1e-12: maxb=0.0
    if S ==0: return inf,0
    score = 2.0*n*maxb/(S*S)
    return score, maxb
```
For trial with same pad, if n<128 uses direct and ignores pad. Fine.

**Optimize stats `fft_stats` should return maxb maybe clipped, S.**
```
def fft_stats(a):
    n=...
    pad=1 << (3*n -2).bit_length()
    Fa=...
    b=irfft(Fa*Fa,pad)[:2*n-1]
    b=np.maximum(b,0.0)
    maxb=b.max(); S=sum; score=...
    return Fa,b,maxb,S,score,pad
```
If n small direct? For gradient need Fa anyway; use FFT for all n>=2. Fine.

def make_y_from_a(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,float)
    # replace zero with tiny relative to max
    amax = np.max(a)
    tiny = max(1e-300, amax*1e-12)
    a_pos = np.where(a > tiny, a, tiny)
    y=np.log(a_pos)
    y=np.mean zero...
    maxabs=max(np.max(np.abs(y)),1.0)
    if maxabs > YMAX: y*=YMAX/maxabs
    return y
```

Detailed functions:
```
def binomial_a(n):
    if n <= 0: return np.array([], dtype=float)
    a=np.empty(n)
    val=1.0
    a[0]=val
    for i in range(1,n):
        val *= (2*i - 1) / (2*i)  # Wait for i index? Let's check: a_i for i=1 from a0: val *= (2*0+1)/(2*0+2)=1/2. If loop i from1: val_prev a_{i-1}, multiply (2*(i-1)+1)/(2*(i-1)+2)=(2i-1)/(2i). Yes.
        a[i]=val
    return a
```

`scan_binomial(max_n=20000)`:
```
val=1.0; S=0.0; best=inf; best_n=1; best_S=1
for n in range(1,max_n+1):
   S += val
   score=2.0*n/(S*S)
   if score<best: best=score; best_n=n
   val *= (2*n -1)/(2*n)  # next a_n if current val a_{n-1}? At n current length, val is a_{n-1}. To get next length n+1, val=a_{n-1} -> a_n: multiply (2*(n-1)+1)/(2*(n-1)+2)=(2n-1)/(2n). Good.
return best_n,best_score
```
Check: start val a0=1, n=1 S=1. after score, val*= (1)/(2)=0.5 a1. n=2 S=1.5. Good.

def insert_seed(top_seeds, y, score, max_count):
   if not np.isfinite(score): return
   if y is None: return
   if len(top_seeds) < max_count:
       top_seeds.append([float(score), np.array(y,float)])
   else:
       worst=0
       worst_score=float('-inf')
       for idx in range(len(top_seeds)):
          if top_seeds[idx][0] > worst_score:
             worst_score=top_seeds[idx][0]; worst=idx
       if score < worst_score:
          top_seeds[worst]=[float(score), np.array(y,float)]
```

`fft_stats`: for n=1? works pad = 1 << (1?). 3*1-2=1 bit_length=1 -> pad=2? Need pad at least 1. Use `size = max(4, 3*n - 2); pad=1 << (size-1).bit_length()`.
```
def fft_stats(a):
    n = len(a)
    L=2*n-1
    size=max(4, 3*n -2)
    pad=1 << (size-1).bit_length()
    Fa=np.fft.rfft(a,pad)
    b=np.fft.irfft(Fa*Fa,pad)[:L]
    b=b.copy() # to modify
    b[np.isnan(b)] = 0? use np.where
    b = np.maximum(b, 0.0)
    maxb=float(np.max(b)) if b.size else 0.0
    S=float(np.sum(a))
    score=2.0*n*maxb/(S*S) if S>1e-300 else float('inf')
    return Fa,b,maxb,S,score,pad
```
`b` may contain tiny imaginary? irfft real.
`b = np.nan_to_num(b, nan=0.0, posinf=1e300, neginf=0.0)`.

`get_G`:
```
def get_G(Fa, w, pad, n):
    if n == 1:
       # w length1, correlation c0 = a? But Fa not enough to recover a? We can avoid n=1 in optimize. For safety return zeros.
       return np.zeros(1)
    L=2*n-1
    Fw=np.fft.rfft(w,pad)
    yconv=np.fft.irfft(Fa*Fw, pad)
    start=L-1
    stop=L-n-1
    c=yconv[start:stop:-1]
    if c.size < n:
       tmp=np.zeros(n); tmp[:c.size]=c; c=tmp
    c=c[:n]
    c=np.nan_to_num(c, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    return 2.0*c
```
If `stop` maybe 0 for n=2, slice works. Good.

`softmax_w`:
```
def softmax_w(b, beta):
    bmax=float(np.max(b)) if b.size else 0.0
    if bmax <= 0: # uniform?
       w=np.ones_like(b)/max(1,b.size); return w,b[?]/?
    beta=max(float(beta), 1e-12 if bmax==0 else bmax*1e-12)
    x=(b - bmax)/beta
    x=np.clip(x, -50.0, 0.0)
    w=np.exp(x)
    s=float(w.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0:
       idx=int(np.argmax(b)); w=np.zeros_like(b); w[idx]=1.0; s=1.0
    else: w/=s
    mw=float(np.dot(w,b))
    if not np.isfinite(mw): mw=bmax
    return w,mw
```

```
    y = np.array(y_seeds[0], dtype=float).copy()
    y = normalize_y(y)
    a = np.exp(y)
    # normalize to max1
    a = normalize_a_max1(a)
    if a is None: a=binomial_a(n)
    score, maxb = score_max_from_a(a)
    best_a=a.copy(); best_score=float(score)
    m=np.zeros(n); v=np.zeros(n); t=0; lr=1.0; max_step=0.25; no_improve=0; seed_idx=0; iter_count=0
    beta_start=0.05; beta_end=0.005
    last_print_time=time.monotonic()
    deadline=time.monotonic()+budget_s
    while time.monotonic()<deadline:
        Fa,b,maxb,S,score,pad = fft_stats(a)
        if not finite(score): break
        if score < best_score - 1e-12:
            best_score=score; best_a=a.copy(); no_improve=0
        # if current much worse than best, reset to best maybe
        if score > best_score * 1.2 and iter_count > 1000:
            a=best_a.copy(); y=make_y_from_a(a)
        # beta progress
        progress = min(1.0, float(iter_count)/20000.0)
        beta_rel = beta_start + (beta_end-beta_start)*progress
        beta = max(1e-8, beta_rel*max(1e-12, maxb))
        w,mw=softmax_w(b,beta)
        G=get_G(Fa,w,pad,n)
        if S <= 0: break
        coeff = 2.0*n/(S*S)
        grad = coeff * (G - 2.0*mw/S)
        gy = a * grad
        gy -= np.mean(gy)
        gy = np.nan_to_num(gy, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
        rms = float(np.sqrt(np.mean(gy*gy)))
        if not np.isfinite(rms) or rms < 1e-15:
            break
        g = gy / rms
        iter_count += 1
        m = 0.9*m + 0.1*g
        v = 0.999*v + 0.001*(g*g)
        bias1 = 1 - 0.9**iter_count
        bias2 = 1 - 0.999**iter_count
        mh = m / bias1
        vh = v / bias2
        step = lr * mh / (np.sqrt(vh) + 1e-8)
        step = np.nan_to_num(step, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
        step -= np.mean(step)
        step_rms = float(np.sqrt(np.mean(step*step)))
        if step_rms > max_step:
            step *= max_step / step_rms
        accepted=False
        # trial factors
        for factor in (1.0, 0.5):
            if time.monotonic() > deadline: break
            a_try = a * np.exp(-step * factor)
            a_try = np.nan_to_num(a_try, nan=0.0, posinf=1e300, neginf=0.0)
            if not np.isfinite(a_try).all() or float(np.sum(a_try)) <= 0:
                a_try = best_a.copy()
                score_try, maxb_try = score_max_from_a(a_try)
            else:
                score_try, maxb_try = score_max_from_a(a_try, pad)
            if not np.isfinite(score_try):
                continue
            if maxb_try > 0:
                a_try = a_try / np.sqrt(maxb_try)
                # after rescale, score_try unchanged scale
            if score_try < score + 1e-10 or score_try < best_score or (score_try < score * 1.02 and rng.random() < 0.05):
                y = make_y_from_a(a_try)
                a = a_try.copy()
                score = score_try
                accepted=True
                break
        if accepted:
            # adapt
            if score < best_score:
                no_improve = 0
            else:
                no_improve += 1
            if no_improve < 200:
                lr = min(2.0, lr * 1.01)
                max_step = min(0.6, max_step + 0.001)
            else:
                lr = max(0.1, lr * 0.95)
            # restart seeds/noise
            if no_improve > 5000:
                time_left = deadline - time.monotonic()
                if seed_idx + 1 < len(y_seeds) and time_left > 300:
                    seed_idx += 1
                    y = np.array(y_seeds[seed_idx], dtype=float).copy()
                    y = normalize_y(y)
                    a = np.exp(y)
                    a = normalize_a_max1(a)
                    if a is None: a=best_a.copy()
                    score, maxb = score_max_from_a(a)
                    m=np.zeros(n); v=np.zeros(n); lr=1.0; max_step=0.25; no_improve=0
                elif time_left > 100:
                    noise = rng.normal(0.0, 0.08, n)
                    y = normalize_y(y + noise)
                    a = np.exp(y)
                    a = normalize_a_max1(a)
                    if a is not None:
                        score, maxb = score_max_from_a(a)
                    no_improve=0
        else:
            no_improve += 1
            if lr > 0.1:
                lr *= 0.8
            if no_improve > 3000:
                # try next seed earlier
                time_left = deadline - time.monotonic()
                if seed_idx + 1 < len(y_seeds) and time_left > 300:
                    seed_idx += 1
                    y = np.array(y_seeds[seed_idx], dtype=float).copy()
                    y = normalize_y(y)
                    a = np.exp(y); a=normalize_a_max1(a)
                    if a is None: a=best_a.copy()
                    score, maxb = score_max_from_a(a)
                    m=np.zeros(n); v=np.zeros(n); lr=1.0; max_step=0.25; no_improve=0
        if time.monotonic() - last_print_time > 30:
            print("n=", n, "iter=", iter_count, "score=", score, "best=", best_score, "lr=", lr, flush=True)
            last_print_time = time.monotonic()
    return best_a, best_score

def make_seed_list(n, parent_a, rng, max_tilts=8):
    top=[]

    # base score known? compute quick maybe via score_max

    y_list=[]

**Subgradient sign check with log update**: We set `y -= step`, step = lr*mh/adapted where m tracks g=grad_y/rms. Since grad_y = ∂F/∂y. To minimize, decrease y in gradient direction. For binomial, gy at a0 positive, step positive, y0 decreases (a0 lower) good. At tail gy negative, step negative, y -= negative -> increases tail. Good.

**In `grad = coeff*(G - 2*mw/S)`, are we missing factor 2?** Let's re-derive with homogeneous. Let F=2n m/S². ∂F/∂a_i = 2n (m_i/S² - 2m/S³), where m_i=Σ w_j ∂b_j/∂a_i = G_i. We defined G_i=2*c_i. Good. `coeff = 2n/S²`; `grad=coeff*(G_i - 2m/S)`. Yes. If using m=mw. Good.
Then ∂F/∂y_i = a_i*grad. Good.

Check sum of grad? For scale invariance, Σ a_i grad_i =0. For smooth weights, Euler: Σ a_i G_i = 2Σ_j w_j b_j = 2mw. Σ a_i * 2m/S = 2m. So Σa_i grad = coeff(2mw-2m)=0. Good. So gy mean not necessarily zero, but we center.

**Potential overflow in `a*np.exp(-step*factor)` if step negative large: exp(0.25)=1.28, fine. Step max 0.6, exp .83. Good.

**Potential underflow if a values tiny and exp positive; okay.

```python
"""
Gradient/exponentiated-search optimizer for nonnegative step-function heights.

The routine searches over several lengths (especially the supplied parent
length when available), starting from flat-autocorrelation-like binomial
coefficients and several tilted varieties.  It uses smoothed max-convolution
gradients in log-space, FFT-based convolution, periodic restarts, and exact
score checks near the end.  The returned sequence is scaled to stay safely
inside the evaluator's clipping range.
"""

import numpy as np
import time

YMAX = 20.0


def binomial_a(n):
    """Coefficients of (1-z)^(-1/2) truncated to length n."""
    if n <= 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
    v = 1.0
    a[0] = v
    for i in range(1, n):
        v *= (2 * i - 1) / (2 * i)
        a[i] = v
    return a


def scan_binomial(max_n=20000):
    """Quickly find a good length for the pure binomial construction."""
    if max_n <= 0:
        return 1, 2.0
    v = 1.0
    s = 0.0
    best = float("inf")
    best_n = 1
    for n in range(1, max_n + 1):
        s += v
        score = (2.0 * n) / (s * s)
        if score < best:
            best = score
            best_n = n
        v *= (2 * n - 1) / (2 * n)
    return best_n, best


def make_y_from_a(a):
    """Convert positive heights to centered, range-limited log coordinates."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    amax = float(np.max(np.abs(a)))
    tiny = max(1e-300, amax * 1e-12)
    a_pos = np.where(a > tiny, a, tiny)
    y = np.log(a_pos)
    y -= np.mean(y)
    y = np.nan_to_num(y, nan=0.0, posinf=YMAX, neginf=-YMAX)
    m = float(np.max(np.abs(y)))
    if m > YMAX and m > 0.0:
        y *= YMAX / m
    return y


def normalize_y(y):
    """Center and range-limit log coordinates."""
    y = np.asarray(y, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if y.size == 0:
        return y
    y -= np.mean(y)
    y = np.nan_to_num(y, nan=0.0, posinf=YMAX, neginf=-YMAX)
    m = float(np.max(np.abs(y)))
    if m > YMAX and m > 0.0:
        y *= YMAX / m
    return y


def fft_stats(a):
    """FFT convolution statistics: Fa, b, max_b, sum, score, pad."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = a.size
    L = 2 * n - 1
    size = max(4, 3 * n - 2)
    pad = 1 << (size - 1).bit_length()
    Fa = np.fft.rfft(a, pad)
    b = np.fft.irfft(Fa * Fa, pad)[:L]
    b = np.asarray(b, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    b = np.nan_to_num(b, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    np.maximum(b, 0.0, out=b)
    maxb = float(np.max(b)) if b.size else 0.0
    S = float(np.sum(a))
    if S > 1e-300:
        score = float(2.0 * n * maxb / (S * S))
    else:
        score = float("inf")
    return Fa, b, maxb, S, score, pad


def score_max_from_a(a, pad=None):
    """Score and maximum convolution coefficient for a candidate sequence."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return float("inf"), 0.0
    S = float(np.sum(a))
    if S <= 1e-300:
        return float("inf"), 0.0
    if n < 128:
        b = np.convolve(a, a)
        b = np.asarray(b, dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        if pad is None:
            size = max(4, 3 * n - 2)
            pad = 1 << (size - 1).bit_length()
        Fa = np.fft.rfft(a, pad)
        b = np.fft.irfft(Fa * Fa, pad)[:2 * n - 1]
        b = np.asarray(b, dtype=np.float64)
    b = np.nan_to_num(b, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    np.maximum(b, 0.0, out=b)
    maxb = float(np.max(b)) if b.size else 0.0
    score = float(2.0 * n * maxb / (S * S))
    return score, maxb


def get_G(Fa, w, pad, n):
    """
    G_i = sum_j w_j * d(b_j)/d(a_i) = 2 * sum_k w_{i+k} a_k.
    """
    if n == 1:
        return np.zeros(1, dtype=np.float64)
    L = 2 * n - 1
    Fw = np.fft.rfft(w, pad)
    yconv = np.fft.irfft(Fa * Fw, pad)
    start = L - 1
    stop = L - n - 1
    c = yconv[start:stop:-1]
    if c.size < n:
        tmp = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        np.copyto(tmp[:c.size], c)
        c = tmp
    c = np.asarray(c[:n], dtype=np.float64).copy()
    c = np.nan_to_num(c, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    return 2.0 * c


def softmax_w(b, beta):
    """Smooth-max weights w and weighted average mw = sum w_j b_j."""
    b = np.asarray(b, dtype=np.float64)
    bmax = float(np.max(b)) if b.size else 0.0
    if bmax <= 0.0:
        w = np.ones_like(b, dtype=np.float64)
        if w.size:
            w /= float(w.size)
        mw = float(np.dot(w, b)) if b.size else 0.0
        return w, mw
    beta = max(float(beta), bmax * 1e-12, 1e-12)
    x = (b - bmax) / beta
    x = np.clip(x, -50.0, 0.0)
    w = np.exp(x)
    s = float(np.sum(w))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        w = np.zeros_like(b, dtype=np.float64)
        idx = int(np.argmax(b))
        w[idx] = 1.0
        mw = bmax
    else:
        w /= s
        mw = float(np.dot(w, b))
        if not np.isfinite(mw):
            mw = bmax
    return w, mw


def insert_seed(top_seeds, y, score, max_count):
    """Maintain a small list of the best seed log-vectors by fast score."""
    if y is None:
        return
    if not np.isfinite(score):
        return
    y_arr = np.asarray(y, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if y_arr.size == 0 or not np.isfinite(y_arr).all():
        return
    if len(top_seeds) < max_count:
        top_seeds.append([float(score), y_arr.copy()])
    else:
        worst_idx = 0
        worst_score = -1e300
        for idx in range(len(top_seeds)):
            cur = float(top_seeds[idx][0])
            if cur > worst_score:
                worst_score = cur
                worst_idx = idx
        if score < worst_score:
            top_seeds[worst_idx] = [float(score), y_arr.copy()]


def normalize_a_max1(a):
    """Scale a sequence so its convolution maximum is 1."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        return None
    score, maxb = score_max_from_a(a)
    if not np.isfinite(score) or maxb <= 0.0:
        return None
    return a / np.sqrt(maxb)


def make_seed_list(n, parent_a, rng, max_tilts=8):
    """Build several good initial log-sequences for a fixed length n."""
    top = []
    if n <= 0:
        return []

    # Binomial seed.
    base = binomial_a(n)
    sb, mb = score_max_from_a(base)
    if np.isfinite(sb):
        yb = make_y_from_a(base)
        insert_seed(top, yb, sb, 6)

    # Parent seed, if compatible.
    if parent_a is not None:
        p = np.asarray(parent_a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        if p.size == n and np.isfinite(p).all():
            sp, mp = score_max_from_a(p)
            if np.isfinite(sp) and mp > 0.0:
                y_p = make_y_from_a(p / np.sqrt(mp))
                insert_seed(top, y_p, sp, 6)

    # Opportunistic tilted/power seeds.
    if max_tilts > 0 and n > 1:
        x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
        idx = np.arange(1, n + 1, dtype=np.float64)
        for _ in range(max_tilts):
            p = float(rng.uniform(0.38, 0.62))
            q1 = float(rng.uniform(-1.2, 1.8))
            q2 = float(rng.uniform(-1.5, 1.5))
            cand = np.power(idx, -p)
            cand *= np.exp(q1 * x + q2 * (x - 0.5))
            cand = np.asarray(cand, dtype=np.float64)
            cand = np.nan_to_num(cand, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
            amax = float(np.max(cand))
            if amax <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(amax):
                continue
            cand /= amax
            sc, mc = score_max_from_a(cand)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and mc > 0.0:
                cand /= np.sqrt(mc)
                y = make_y_from_a(cand)
                insert_seed(top, y, sc, 6)

        # A few deterministic tilts of the binomial seed.
        for factor in (-1.2, -0.6, 0.6, 1.2, 1.8):
            cand = base * np.exp(0.5 * factor * x)
            cand = np.asarray(cand, dtype=np.float64)
            cand = np.nan_to_num(cand, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
            amax = float(np.max(cand))
            if amax <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(amax):
                continue
            cand /= amax
            sc, mc = score_max_from_a(cand)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and mc > 0.0:
                cand /= np.sqrt(mc)
                y = make_y_from_a(cand)
                insert_seed(top, y, sc, 6)

    if not top:
        top.append([float("inf"), make_y_from_a(base)])

    out = []
    for item in top:
        out.append(item[1])
    return out


def optimize_n(n, budget_s, y_seeds, rng):
    """Optimize length n for the supplied budget."""
    if n <= 0:
        return None, float("inf")
    if n == 1:
        a = binomial_a(1)
        return a, 2.0

    if y_seeds is None or len(y_seeds) == 0:
        y_seeds = [make_y_from_a(binomial_a(n))]

    y = np.asarray(y_seeds[0], dtype=np.float64).copy()
    y = normalize_y(y)
    a = np.exp(np.clip(y, -YMAX, YMAX))
    a_norm = normalize_a_max1(a)
    if a_norm is None:
        a = binomial_a(n)
    else:
        a = a_norm
    score, _ = score_max_from_a(a)
    best_a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    best_score = float(score)

    m = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    v = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    it = 0
    lr = 1.0
    max_step = 0.25
    no_improve = 0
    seed_idx = 0
    last_print = time.monotonic()

    beta_start = 0.05
    beta_end = 0.005
    deadline = time.monotonic() + max(0.0, float(budget_s))

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        Fa, b, maxb, S, score, pad = fft_stats(a)
        if not np.isfinite(score):
            break
        if score < best_score - 1e-12:
            best_score = float(score)
            best_a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).copy()
            no_improve = 0

        # Defensive reset if the current point has drifted badly.
        if score > best_score * 1.25 and it > 1000:
            a = best_a.copy()

        progress = min(1.0, float(it) / 20000.0)
        beta_rel = beta_start + (beta_end - beta_start) * progress
        beta = max(1e-8, beta_rel * max(1e-12, maxb))
        w, mw = softmax_w(b, beta)
        G = get_G(Fa, w, pad, n)
        if S <= 0.0:
            break
        coeff = 2.0 * n / (S * S)
        grad = coeff * (G - 2.0 * mw / S)
        gy = a * grad
        gy = np.nan_to_num(gy, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
        gy -= np.mean(gy)
        rms = float(np.sqrt(np.mean(gy * gy)))
        if not np.isfinite(rms) or rms < 1e-15:
            break
        g = gy / rms

        it += 1
        m = 0.9 * m + 0.1 * g
        v = 0.999 * v + 0.001 * (g * g)
        bias1 = 1.0 - (0.9 ** it)
        bias2 = 1.0 - (0.999 ** it)
        mh = m / bias1
        vh = v / bias2
        step = lr * mh / (np.sqrt(vh) + 1e-8)
        step = np.nan_to_num(step, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
        step -= np.mean(step)
        step_rms = float(np.sqrt(np.mean(step * step)))
        if step_rms > max_step and step_rms > 0.0:
            step *= max_step / step_rms

        accepted = False
        for factor in (1.0, 0.5):
            if time.monotonic() > deadline:
                break
            a_try = a * np.exp(-step * factor)
            a_try = np.nan_to_num(a_try, nan=0.0, posinf=1e300, neginf=0.0)
            if not np.isfinite(a_try).all() or float(np.sum(a_try)) <= 0.0:
                continue
            score_try, maxb_try = score_max_from_a(a_try, pad)
            if not np.isfinite(score_try) or maxb_try <= 0.0:
                continue
            a_try = a_try / np.sqrt(maxb_try)
            if (score_try < score + 1e-10) or (score_try < best_score) or \
               (score_try < score * 1.02 and rng.random() < 0.05):
                y = make_y_from_a(a_try)
                a = np.asarray(a_try, dtype=np.float64).copy()
                score = float(score_try)
                accepted = True
                break

        if accepted:
            if score < best_score:
                no_improve = 0
            else:
                no_improve += 1
            if no_improve < 200:
                lr = min(2.0, lr * 1.01)
                max_step = min(0.60, max_step + 0.001)
            else:
                lr = max(0.1, lr * 0.95)
            if no_improve > 5000:
                time_left = deadline - time.monotonic()
                if seed_idx + 1 < len(y_seeds) and time_left > 300:
                    seed_idx += 1
                    y = np.asarray(y_seeds[seed_idx], dtype=np.float64).copy()
                    y = normalize_y(y)
                    a = np.exp(np.clip(y, -YMAX, YMAX))
                    a_norm = normalize_a_max1(a)
                    if a_norm is not None:
                        a = a_norm
                    else:
                        a = best_a.copy()
                    score, _ = score_max_from_a(a)
                    m = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
                    v = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
                    lr = 1.0
                    max_step = 0.25
                    no_improve = 0
                elif time_left > 100:
                    nz = rng.normal(0.0, 0.08, n)
                    y = normalize_y(y + nz)
                    a = np.exp(np.clip(y, -YMAX, YMAX))
                    a_norm = normalize_a_max1(a)
                    if a_norm is not None:
                        a = a_norm
                        score, _ = score_max_from_a(a)
                    no_improve = 0
        else:
            no_improve += 1
            if lr > 0.1:
                lr *= 0.8
            if no_improve > 3000:
                time_left = deadline - time.monotonic()
                if seed_idx + 1 < len(y_seeds) and time_left > 300:
                    seed_idx += 1
                    y = np.asarray(y_seeds[seed_idx], dtype=np.float64).copy()
                    y = normalize_y(y)
                    a = np.exp(np.clip(y, -YMAX, YMAX))
                    a_norm = normalize_a_max1(a)
                    if a_norm is not None:
                        a = a_norm
                    else:
                        a = best_a.copy()
                    score, _ = score_max_from_a(a)
                    m = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
                    v = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
                    lr = 1.0
                    max_step = 0.25
                    no_improve = 0

        if time.monotonic() - last_print > 30.0:
            print("optimize n=%d it=%d score=%.10g best=%.10g lr=%.4g" %
                  (n, it, score, best_score, lr), flush=True)
            last_print = time.monotonic()

    return best_a, best_score


def finalize_sequence(a):
    """Return a valid Python list scaled safely inside [0, 1000]."""
    fallback = binomial_a(64)
    if a is None:
        a = fallback
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0 or not np.isfinite(a).all():
        a = fallback
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, None)
    a = np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    mx = float(np.max(a))
    if not np.isfinite(mx) or mx <= 0.0:
        a = fallback
        mx = float(np.max(a))
    out = (a / mx) * 500.0
    out = np.asarray(out, dtype=np.float64)
    return [float(x) for x in out]


def exact_score_seq(seq):
    """Call the authoritative evaluator with a safeguard."""
    try:
        val = evaluate_sequence(seq)
        return float(val)
    except Exception:
        return float("inf")


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    """Main search driver."""
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    try:
        budget_s = float(budget_s)
    except Exception:
        budget_s = 2400.0
    if budget_s <= 0:
        budget_s = 2400.0

    start = time.monotonic()
    deadline = start + max(0.0, budget_s - 15.0)

    best_a = None
    best_score = float("inf")

    parent_a = None
    try:
        parent_raw = height_sequence_1
    except Exception:
        parent_raw = None

    if parent_raw is not None:
        p = np.asarray(parent_raw, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        if p.size > 0 and np.isfinite(p).all():
            p = np.clip(p, 0.0, 1e6)
            if p.size <= 20000:
                sp, _ = score_max_from_a(p)
                if np.isfinite(sp):
                    parent_a = p
                    best_score = sp
                    best_a = p

    # Choose lengths to inspect.
    max_search_n = 12000
    bin_best_n, _ = scan_binomial(min(max_search_n, 20000))

    n_list = []
    preferred = []

    if parent_a is not None:
        ln = int(parent_a.size)
        if 1 <= ln <= max_search_n:
            preferred.append(ln)

    preferred.append(4806 if 4806 <= max_search_n else bin_best_n)
    preferred.append(bin_best_n)
    preferred.append(max(2, int(bin_best_n * 0.5)))
    preferred.append(min(max_search_n, int(bin_best_n * 2)))
    for z in (256, 512, 1024, 2048, 4096, 8192, 12000):
        if z <= max_search_n:
            preferred.append(z)

    for z in preferred:
        z = int(z)
        if 2 <= z <= max_search_n and z not in n_list:
            n_list.append(z)

    if not n_list:
        n_list = [4806 if 4806 <= max_search_n else 512]

    # Primary allocation: prefer parent / 4806.
    left = deadline - time.monotonic() - 20.0
    if left > 30.0:
        primary_alloc = min(1400.0, max(60.0, left * 0.55))
        n0 = n_list[0]
        max_tilts = 10 if n0 < 1500 else (6 if n0 < 5000 else 4)
        if budget_s < 180:
            max_tilts = 2
        seeds0 = make_seed_list(n0, parent_a, rng, max_tilts=max_tilts)
        a_loc, s_loc = optimize_n(n0, primary_alloc, seeds0, rng)
        if a_loc is not None and np.isfinite(s_loc) and s_loc < best_score:
            best_score = float(s_loc)
            best_a = np.asarray(a_loc, dtype=np.float64).copy()
        print("primary n=%d local_best=%.10g global=%.10g" %
              (n0, s_loc, best_score), flush=True)

    # Secondary allocations.
    left = deadline - time.monotonic() - 30.0
    for idx in range(1, len(n_list)):
        if left < 90.0:
            break
        n = n_list[idx]
        alloc = min(350.0, max(60.0, left * 0.45))
        if time.monotonic() + alloc > deadline - 20.0:
            alloc = deadline - time.monotonic() - 20.0
        if alloc < 30.0:
            break
        max_tilts = 8 if n < 1500 else (5 if n < 5000 else 3)
        seeds = make_seed_list(n, parent_a, rng, max_tilts=max_tilts)
        a_loc, s_loc = optimize_n(n, alloc, seeds, rng)
        if a_loc is not None and np.isfinite(s_loc) and s_loc < best_score:
            best_score = float(s_loc)
            best_a = np.asarray(a_loc, dtype=np.float64).copy()
        print("secondary n=%d local_best=%.10g global=%.10g time_left=%.1f" %
              (n, s_loc, best_score, deadline - time.monotonic()), flush=True)

    # Exact verification on a few short/medium candidates.
    exact_best_seq = None
    exact_best_val = float("inf")

    candidates = []
    if best_a is not None:
        candidates.append(np.asarray(best_a, dtype=np.float64).ravel())
    if parent_a is not None:
        candidates.append(parent_a)
    candidates.append(binomial_a(4806 if len(n_list) == 0 or n_list[0] != 4806 else n_list[0]))

    checked_len = []
    for cand in candidates:
        c = np.asarray(cand, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        if c.size < 2 or c.size > 12000:
            continue
        if c.size in checked_len:
            continue
        checked_len.append(c.size)
        seq = finalize_sequence(c)
        sv = exact_score_seq(seq)
        if np.isfinite(sv) and sv < exact_best_val:
            exact_best_val = float(sv)
            exact_best_seq = seq

    if exact_best_seq is not None:
        print("final exact best=%.10g" % exact_best_val, flush=True)
        return exact_best_seq

    # Fallback.
    if best_a is None:
        best_a = binomial_a(64)
    seq = finalize_sequence(best_a)
    sv = exact_score_seq(seq)
    if np.isfinite(sv):
        return seq

    # Last-resort valid sequence.
    return finalize_sequence(np.asarray(best_a, dtype=np.float64).ravel() if best_a is not None else binomial_a(64))
```
